RESPLE2 · GPA-VGGT · OctWorld：时序状态、几何效率与可验证边界

覆盖时间：2026-10-24 16:17:58 至 2026-11-01 22:59:32（Asia/Shanghai）。本期按 .cache/slam-3d-state.json 上次覆盖结束时间继续推进，未截断为最近 7 天。

Executive Summary

连续时间状态的设计决定传感器融合边界。RESPLE2以端点clamping和状态/协方差重置连接样条与IMU传播；SVIn+把声呐、压力等约束放入水下局部里程计。返航距离与全轨迹误差须分别判断。

前馈几何开始按层、时间与可信度分工。GPA-VGGT保留Q、按层处理K/V；HorizonStream将局部匹配与循环几何状态分开；Any3R用已标定射线统一相机。失败探测与性能加速分别需要独立评测。

世界生成的记忆结构可以量化，但不能直接当作SLAM。OctWorld用自适应OctMap重投影维护静态空间记忆；模型1.5总分提高的同时部分几何指标下降，且相机路径作为输入。

RESPLE2: Inertial-Informed Recursive Non-Uniform Spline Estimation for Multi-Sensor Odometry with Global Refinement

new · 2610.37602v1

v1 2026-10-27T17:33:19Z
updated 2026-10-27T17:33:19Z

Fig. 2 · 原图已视觉确认

剧烈运动下的异步多传感器连续时间估计。

非均匀 B 样条表示轨迹，以 IMU 递归预测端点，再融合激光、视觉和足端测量。回环全局修正单独运行。

端点钳制使控制点对应物理状态。解除钳制时同步变换状态和协方差。

GrandTour ARC-3 训练段，RESPLE2-LKVIO 的 APE RMSE 为 0.060 m，Coco-LIC LVIO 为 0.154 m，两者传感器组合不同。HOPPER flip1 外置 IMU 下的速度 RMSE 为 0.227 m/s，RESPLE 为 0.789 m/s。

按 IMU 配置复现预测消融，分开统计局部估计与全局优化成本。

LingGS: Decoupling Feed-Forward Geometry Prediction from Camera Tracking for High-Fidelity Gaussian SLAM

new · 2610.36853v1

v1 2026-10-27T08:51:00Z
updated 2026-10-27T08:51:00Z

Figure 1 · 原图已视觉确认

单目高斯建图需要一致深度和稳定的相机轨迹。

冻结 LingBot-Map 生成虚拟 RGB-D，用 ORB-SLAM3 特征跟踪和局部 BA 求位姿，再更新高斯地图。

直接深度残差的权重约按 z⁻² 衰减，避免虚拟右图残差的像素与深度耦合。

Oxford 六段中，直接深度求解器的 ATE 为 73.8 cm，原相机头为 598.5 cm。跟踪耗时从 9.5 增至 16.1 ms，轨迹经 Sim(3) 对齐。

复现时固定帧与对齐协议。论文未处理回环和动态场景。

SVIn+: Multi-Modal Framework for Robust Underwater State Estimation using 3D Sonar, Visual-Inertial, Water-Pressure, and DVL

new · 2610.36454v1

v1 2026-10-27T01:01:19Z
updated 2026-10-27T01:01:19Z

Fig. 2 · 原图已视觉确认

浑浊、弱光和声呐稀疏条件下的水下里程计。

滑窗联合优化视觉重投影、IMU、压力深度、DVL 速度和声呐相对运动。

压力约束竖直方向，DVL 提供速度。声呐相对运动因子无需两个世界坐标系对齐。

标记回访空间偏差为 0.21 ± 0.08 m，SVIn2 为 0.53 ± 0.11 m。返航端点距离另为 0.52 对 0.81 m。实验没有全局轨迹真值。

复现前核对压力残差和传感器外参，分别评估标记回访与返航。

GPA-VGGT: Geometry-Preserving Acceleration of VGGT

new · 2610.37000v2

v1 2026-10-27T10:46:52Z
updated 2026-10-28T08:53:15Z

S4.F4 · 原图已视觉确认

降低 VGGT 长序列推理成本，同时保留精细位姿精度。

早期层共享跨帧 K/V 摘要。后期保留全部 Q，仅合并 K/V，几何敏感层降低合并比例。

给合并后的键加 log(s) 修正，其中 s 为该键代表的 token 数。随机投影只用于匹配。

200 帧 CO3D 的 AUC@3 为 61.43%，原 VGGT 为 61.47%。另一项 200 帧基准中，RTX A6000 上全前向 33.65 对 86.78 秒，约 2.58 倍。

已有源码静态审计。复现需分长度测试，各配置独立启动进程，另测显存。

Does VGGT Know What It Does Not Know?

new · 2610.35806v1

v1 2026-10-26T17:37:39Z
updated 2026-10-26T17:37:39Z		方法正文位置

未发现经确认的总览图，不嵌结果图。

判断模型能否发现图像对的相对位姿估计失败。

冻结 VGGT，从内部表征训练线性探针，预测图像对的位姿失败风险。

将位姿失败作为图像对级事件，对照原生点图置信度及使用相同监督信号的输出分类器。

HardMatch 903 对样本的分组交叉验证中，探针 ROC-AUC 为 93.5%，点图置信度为 79.7%。失败定义为中位极线距离超过 50 px。

探针依赖监督标签。落地前需验证跨域拒绝率与位姿后端的实际收益。

Any3R: Feedforward 3D Reconstruction from Any Camera Type

new · 2610.36150v1

v1 2026-10-26T18:53:14Z
updated 2026-10-26T18:53:14Z

S2.F3 · 原图已视觉确认

用一个前馈模型处理透视、鱼眼和全景等已标定的中央相机。

以标定射线角编码位置，预测径向距离和位姿，再以 X=dR 还原三维点。

按像素对应的球面面积加权训练，校正不同投影对方向的采样密度。

室内 ERP 360° 三视图中，点云 Acc 为 0.054 m，PanoVGGT 为 0.072 m。结果采用 Sim(3) 对齐及 ICP。

需要准确的标定射线图。复现时对齐输入条件并测量标定误差敏感性。

HorizonStream: Long-Horizon Attention for Streaming 3D Reconstruction

revised · 2605.23889v2

v1 2026-05-22T17:50:48Z
updated 2026-10-24T14:53:26Z

S3.F3 · 原图已视觉确认

流式重建需在长序列中保存几何信息并控制状态大小。

门控窗口做局部匹配，递归注意力保存长期状态，尺度读出稳定深度和位移。回环是可选模块。

不同通道按输入学习记忆衰减率。核心状态张量固定，回环索引与输出地图的资源另计。

KITTI 11 条全序列平均 ATE 为 19.75 m，LingBot-Map 为 25.29 m，启用回环后为 16.44 m。失败项以该序列最差值计入。

先评估无回环的流式协议。部分训练与测试集重叠，泛化结果需分组判断。

OctWorld: Long-Range World-Consistent Video Generation with Octree-Based 3D Mapping

revised · 2609.03919v2

v1 2026-09-03T14:35:38Z
updated 2026-10-27T11:49:31Z

S3.F2 · 原图已视觉确认

让给定相机轨迹的视频生成在重访时保留场景结构。

将生成的 RGB-D 融合进八叉树 TSDF，并向未来视点渲染几何条件，配合压缩视频历史生成下一段。

用像素足迹决定体素层级，加权融合距离和颜色。地图节点会随探索增加。

Re10K 80 段重访中，模型 1.0 的 LPIPS 为 0.1633，Gen3C 为 0.1933。归一化点云 L1 Chamfer 为 0.0345 对 0.0457。

模型 1.5 仍依赖 DA3 估深。模型 1.0 首段全流程速度为 0.136 FPS，地图模块需单独计时。

展开阅读证据
RESPLE2 · 完整阅读证据

解决的问题

异步传感器在激烈运动下需要按原始时间建模；一般B样条控制点不在轨迹上，无法直接套IMU运动学预测。滑窗非均匀样条还可能等待未来IMU而延迟。 正文证据, 正文证据, 正文证据, 正文证据, 正文证据

方法 / 结构

  • 三次非均匀B样条拆分平移R3和旋转S3。局部4个6维控制点加acc/gyro bias共30维；IMU传播临时增3维速度形成33维状态。 正文证据, 正文证据, 正文证据
  • 同一knot区间IMU样本交替分成互斥prediction/update两组。以endpoint multiplicity=3实现clamp，使s(t_n)=s_n、r(t_n)=r_n，可沿IMU strapdown直接预测控制点。 正文证据, 正文证据, 正文证据, 正文证据, 正文证据
  • 新knot到来会unclamp上一个端点，改变basis。平移closed-form reset保持旧端点位置/速度/加速度；旋转Newton reset保持方向/角速率，通常5迭代；同时P+=J P- J^T变换含bias交叉项的联合协方差。 正文证据, 正文证据, 正文证据, 正文证据, 正文证据, 正文证据, 正文证据
  • 逐测量时间插值得到LiDAR点面、IMU加速度/角速率、KLT视觉重投影和足端速度/触地平面残差，堆叠后IEKF迭代。VoxelMap维护平面和点噪声；加速度/视觉用Huber。视觉地图点深度来自LiDAR；足端因子假设接触无滑移。 正文证据, 正文证据, 正文证据, 正文证据, 正文证据
  • knot由信息量/方向平衡、最长间隔、位移/旋转/速度变化共同触发；几何矩阵G=Σ nn^T/σ²的最小特征值与最小值/trace检测弱方向。全局由iBTC生成回环，LM直接优化控制点而非先离散posegraph再拟合spline。 正文证据, 正文证据, 正文证据

Insight 与数学知识

  • clamp把控制变量与物理运动状态在端点精确等同：s(t_n)=s_n；v(t_n)=3(s_n-s_{n-1})/τ；ω(t_n)=6δ_n/τ。这里的exact是样条参数化/端点传播关系，不是无噪声真值保证。 正文证据, 正文证据, 正文证据
  • unclamp重置不是额外观测更新，而是basis变化下的状态坐标变换，因此必须同步重置协方差；orientation reset的Jacobian由隐函数零残差条件得到。 正文证据, 正文证据
  • 全局目标由回环和相邻控制点相对里程计残差组成；后者只连接两个控制点，回环才用spline interpolation，维持稀疏性。保持高阶运动一致性是软约束效果，正文Fig11展示速度保持。 正文证据, 正文证据, 正文证据, 正文证据

datasets
GrandTour(ANYmal D)、JEPLO(Unitree Go2)、PPS tracked vehicle、HOPPER飞腿四旋翼、HANDI手持13段。HOPPER有12段，最高约25g/18rad/s；external BMI088±24g 1000/500Hz，对比internal ICM40609±4g 200Hz。
ground_truth
GrandTour benchmark/公开GT；HOPPER 10台Vicon在10×10m房间提供300Hz pose、100Hz velocity，long段只起止在房间；HANDI长段部分仅起止Qualisys，az-out1/2只报告end-to-end。
metrics
evo APE/RPE RMSE以m计；RPE固定0.5m路径长度间隔、point-distance pose relation；失败✗定义误差>50m。速度Table VI单位PDF确认m/s，HTML漏掉/s。
hardware
Ubuntu24.04，AMD Ryzen9 9950X，32GB RAM。ROS2双节点里程计/全局优化，每节点通常5 OpenMP线程；Eigen/Pinocchio/CHOLMOD。
baseline_fairness
Coco-LIC、FAST-LIVO2、CTE-MLO、Point-LIO、Leg-KILO；RESPLE基线改用相同VoxelMap以减小地图噪声差异。GrandTour六test段他方法数值取原文且使用更高端HG4930 IMU，RESPLE2使用Mid360内置IMU，不能作完全相同传感器对比；四train段均内置IMU。每数据集/传感器配置共用一套参数。

复现与启发

先复现HOPPER同knot预测消融，再复核clamp/unclamp前后kinematics和协方差一致性；按external/internalIMU分组，区分局部odom帧与异步global总成本。主线程已审RESPLE2源码，本次不重复源码审计。
  • 无独立Limitations章节；Conclusion明确未来要自适应sensor weighting、摆脱LiDAR-derived depth的视觉约束和与规划集成。 正文证据
  • 视觉需要前帧LiDAR深度；KLT受纹理、足端无滑移假设受接触误判/滑移影响；可靠外参与时间同步仍必要。 正文证据, 正文证据, 正文证据, 正文证据
  • 图优化用iBTC成功回环作输入；未给错误回环鲁棒性系统评估、长期全局内存上限或机载闭环控制延迟验证。未从约2s异步全局solve推导硬实时保证。 正文证据, 正文证据
  • GrandTour test异IMU、HOPPER/HANDI部分GT/端点误差协议须保留；桌面5线程每节点性能不等于小型机载平台表现。 正文证据, 正文证据, 正文证据

逐项证据与原文定位
LingGS · 完整阅读证据

解决的问题

单目Gaussian SLAM同时缺深度和准确pose。学习模型的depth有用，但其camera head误差会让多帧Gaussian相互冲突；单帧深度还可能尺度不一致。 正文证据, 正文证据

方法 / 结构

  • 冻结LingBot-Map用最初RGB帧估计并固定intrinsics K，后续输出一致深度作为虚拟RGB-D；没有新增训练。 正文证据, 正文证据
  • 保留ORB-SLAM3提取/匹配/local BA；每帧1200 ORB、8层金字塔，匹配局部map后固定map点优化SE3 pose。四轮优化/离群点重分类，Huber鲁棒核。 正文证据, 正文证据
  • 残差为(u误差,v误差,直接depth误差)，Σ=diag(σ²,σ²,(0.05z)²)。取代RGB-D虚拟右图像残差并改对应Jacobian和权重。 正文证据, 正文证据, 正文证据, 正文证据
  • Gaussian插入权由当前图像Laplacian超过render的正差决定；有效depth反投影初始化center，尺度2z/fx、opacity0.07、RGB颜色。每keyframe30步，0.2专门采最新，其余在全部keyframes均匀replay。 正文证据, 正文证据, 正文证据
  • 优化0.8L1_RGB+0.2(1-SSIM)+0.01L1_inverse_depth，gsplat渲染；depth先验始终是模型预测而非GT深度。 正文证据, 正文证据

Insight 与数学知识

  • 虚拟右图像残差r_R=r_u+(fx b/(z_obs z_pred))r_z，因此成本有u-depth交叉项；局部深度平方项系数约z^-4。direct depth按恒定5%相对不确定性给z^-2权重，改变远近深度的约束分配，不能说原ORB-SLAM3信息矩阵显式有z^-4。 正文证据, 正文证据, 正文证据, 正文证据, 正文证据, 正文证据
  • mapper用inverse-depth L1，近似|Δz|/z²，同样更重近处；Laplacian插入是缺失细节启发式，不覆盖所有photometric disagreement。 正文证据, 正文证据

datasets
7-Scenes七场景；Oxford Spires六段1000–2001帧(Keble02/03/04、Observatory01、ChristChurch02、Bodleian02)；RE10K选择60段71–279帧；TUM-D选择11段。所有518×392输入。

metrics
held-out NVS PSNR/SSIM/LPIPS；ATE RMSE先Sim3 Umeyama对齐；连续帧RPE_t(cm)/RPE_R(deg)；Table2为scale-aligned rendereddepth meanL1(m)。端到端FPS=帧数/总重建时间。
hardware
单NVIDIA RTX4090 GPU+AMD Ryzen Threadripper PRO3995WX 64-core CPU。
baseline_fairness
MonoGS/S3PO-GS/Photo-SLAM/OTF公开实现相同输入帧；Photo-SLAM mapping从8提高到最多200迭代以约6FPS加强画质，非其默认配置；Photo-SLAM Pumpkin失败故7Scenes平均仅6场景。OTF只在有pose的keyframe/heldout子集算轨迹误差，不同于全输入帧。ICP消融是Open3D CPU未优化参考，不能宣称ICP计算极限。

复现与启发

先保留相同frame/keyframe/GT对齐协议复现Table3四个pose源，单独统计depth推理、CPU tracking和mapper；直接深度与虚拟stereo差别可作为不重训的简洁适配点。视觉质量与metric geometry分开评估，保留每集depth valid mask。
  • Conclusion明说未处理loop closure和dynamic scenes；ORB在重复/弱纹理退化。不是可长期闭环稠密SLAM的已验证方案。 正文证据
  • 误差经过Sim3及depth尺度对齐，不能当未知尺度单目绝对metric能力。固定5%depth噪声是假设，未见模型分布/动态退化的校准验证。 正文证据, 正文证据, 正文证据
  • 代码/config承诺录用后发布；正文没给initial frames数量n0、完整keyframe选取阈值、held-out拆分细节、长期缓存/显存增长统计。只做跨主干单序列实验不能支持全部场景泛化。 正文证据, 正文证据, 正文证据, 正文证据, 正文证据
  • 约7FPS来自单4090+64核desktop的总序列吞吐，不等于30Hz在线输入时无积压/机载实测。 正文证据, 正文证据

逐项证据与原文定位
SVIn+ · 完整阅读证据

解决的问题

水下视觉受浑浊/光照/回散影响；压力/DVL能约束深度/速度却不提供环境结构。3D声呐5Hz、稀疏且多路径噪声明显，需要低成本地加入估计器。 正文证据, 正文证据, 正文证据

方法 / 结构

  • 基于SVIn2/OKVIS slidingwindow非线性最小二乘，紧耦合camera重投影、IMU预积分、压力深度、DVL速度。传感器外参预先标定且固定。 正文证据, 正文证据, 正文证据, 正文证据
  • 压力标定water-surface offset并约束globalZ，假定短实验水面高度/潮汐/环境压力不变；DVL在sensor坐标量测速度，经外参/杠杆臂角速度项变换，采用sensor自带协方差作information。 正文证据, 正文证据, 正文证据, 正文证据
  • 3D声呐以KISS-ICP做scan-to-local-map，但motionprior来自上一SVIn+而非原constantvelocity；声呐world与融合world分离。将相邻声呐pose作SE3插值对齐状态时刻，构造6D相对位姿因子。 正文证据, 正文证据, 正文证据, 正文证据, 正文证据
  • 因此应称视觉/惯性/压力/DVL紧耦合+3D声呐分层或松耦合，不能称raw sonar point factors端到端全紧耦合。Fig2突出局部optimizer；论文末尾把multimodal global place recognition列为未来工作。 正文证据, 正文证据, 正文证据

Insight 与数学知识

  • 总目标Σe_r^TΩ_re_r+e_IMU^TΩ_IMUe_IMU+e_DVL^TΩ_DVLe_DVL+Ω_depth e_depth²+e_sonar^TΩ_sonar e_sonar保持稀疏；用relative sonar motion消除两个world frame的绝对gauge差异。 正文证据, 正文证据, 正文证据, 正文证据
  • DVL必须加角速度×安装位置的杠杆臂修正，压力也需考虑sensor offset随姿态变化；简单把DVL当body velocity/压力当IMU高度会引入系统误差。 正文证据, 正文证据
  • 论文Eq7虽然介绍当前z_d,k，却只写D0−predicted sensorZ，且D0只用初始z_d,0定义；当前压力量测项未显式出现，HTML与PDF均如此。这是需代码/作者澄清的公式歧义，不据此断言实现错误。 正文证据, 正文证据, 正文证据

复现与启发

优先核清pressure Eq7与实际sensor offset/符号；按tag三次重访分别算start-end和TableI空间误差，保留无GT边界；加入sonar稀疏/无回波失败门控和因子相关性检查，再测完整同步/插值延迟。README仅静态阅读，未编译、未下数据。
  • 独立Limitations节没有，VI Lessons Learned/Future Work明确：传感器退化主动切换、global multimodal relocalization/loopclosure、在线外参标定均未来方向。 正文证据
  • 无全局GT、仅有限水下三数据集、长序列距离写法矛盾；不能由定性map coherence推断绝对全局一致或误差有界。 正文证据, 正文证据, 正文证据
  • 声呐odom依赖融合pose prior而后又回灌相对因子；全文没有说明如何处理相关性/registration covariance或失败门控参数，需要实现验证，不能假设因子独立。 正文证据, 正文证据, 正文证据
  • 公式7当前深度项书写含糊；window大小K、信息矩阵具体数值/sonar插值缓冲延迟、数据split/下载入口及完整runtime表未给，复现需要补核。 正文证据, 正文证据, 正文证据, 正文证据
  • 获取时作者cmburgul项目页存在，可读paper/code链接，但Dataset与arXiv仍comingsoon、MoreWorks仍模板占位；第二dartmouth项目页基本空白。GitHub README有未解<<<<<<< HEAD标记、Running ToDo，虽给ROS2Jazzy启动例，不能据此说开箱即用。网页/README于2026-11-02获取，不证明冻结截止时完全相同。 正文证据, 正文证据, 正文证据

逐项证据与原文定位
GPA-VGGT · 完整阅读证据

解决的问题

对VGGT压缩attention时，宽松AUC@30会掩盖精细位姿下降：合并query使多像素共享输出、未恢复token数量破坏attention mass、早期完全去掉跨帧上下文使不同视图发生不一致表征漂移。目标是在不训练/微调的条件下保留严格几何精度。

方法 / 结构

  • 早期global blocks 0–8改为帧attention，但每个query读取本帧patch、全部帧camera+4 registers和内容自适应patch摘要；stride-8 seeds后按head级cosine聚类，跨帧KV集只构造一次。
  • 后期blocks 9–23保留全部Q，仅合并K/V，通常merge ratio 0.9；13–16核心块用0.2，因为其中稀疏跨视图匹配更易被均值稀释。
  • 对每个merged/pooled key按代表的原token数量s给logit加log(s)，恢复attention质量。
  • 1024→128固定Gaussian随机投影只用于matching argmax；原K/V仍按选定分组平均。Triton注意力核直接加入per-key bias，matching核不物化完整score矩阵。

Insight 与数学知识

  • claim
    完整Q、压缩KV的输出为 o_i=Σ_j softmax_j(q_iᵀ k̄_j/√d + log s_j) v̄_j。当组内keys完全相同时，与未合并attention精确相等；一般keys相近时仍为近似，不是无损证明。
    source
    S4.E1; S4.SS1.p2.1–p2.2
  • claim
    保留Q把attention复杂度从Nmerged²提高为N×Nmerged，主动保留每token独立预测；节省的是K/V轴的计算，而不是任意压缩所有token。
    source
    S4.SS1.p1.1
  • claim
    log(s)是概率质量修正而非学习bias；去掉后首帧接收近两倍attention。两个算子消融中，mass修正带来的AUC恢复大于单独保Q。
    source
    S3.SS1; A3.T3 / Appendix D
  • claim
    128D随机投影只需选到足够相似的近似邻居，无需完全保留argmax。只有76.6%分组目的地相同，但相似度损失0.56%；这是一组实测权衡，不是跨数据集精度保证。
    source
    Appendix F, Table S4
  • claim
    核心块按特殊token最终输出的相对Frobenius偏离选出，不使用GT任务标签；多块交互使逐层排名不等价于最优块集。
    source
    Appendix E

  • AVGGT官方代码未公开，作者据规范重实现；其原RE10K子集/采样未公开，不能把本文绝对AUC与AVGGT原论文绝对数直接比较。

  • 200帧2.58倍并非所有长度都成立：10帧0.78对0.84秒，仅约1.08倍；最快AVGGT4在长序列更快但精度损失大。
  • 峰值显存没有随attention加速而大降；不应称几何精度与内存双重数量级改善。

    逐项证据与原文定位
    VGGT 失败探测 · 完整阅读证据

    解决的问题

    VGGT和pi3输出的是每像素几何confidence，未提供相机相对位姿是否正确的可靠性。研究问题是冻结表示是否已线性编码自身失败，以及该信息能否跨类别/数据/失败模式使用。

    方法 / 结构

    • 冻结VGGT1B，图对单次前向。RGB最长边518、短边14倍数，居中方形画布；从pose编码解码R,t,K。pi3不预测intrinsics，按local pointmap最小二乘拟合focal。
    • HardMatch无GTpose，以人工匹配的对称极线距离中位数定义失败；剔除dynamic及<8对应，留下903对97类，主阈值50px。PhotoTourism/ScanNet按GT旋转/平移方向定义失败。
    • hook encoder、aggregator、camera token/trunk；每图mean pool，再两图mean，标准化及PCA128，带L2和balanced class weights的logistic probe。
    • 10-fold按HardMatch类别分组，内层5-fold选正则；1000次整类别bootstrap给95%CI；transfer在全HardMatch训练后完全不重训。与point/depth confidence及同CV训练的all-output classifier比较。

    Insight 与数学知识

    • claim
      预测fundamental matrix为 F=H2ᵀ K2^-ᵀ[t]× R K1^-1 H1；H把原图坐标映到network canvas，避免直接把缩放padding后的相机参数用于原图对应。HardMatch标签混合旋转、平移、intrinsics错误，不能等同纯旋转失败。
      source
      S3.E1; S3.SS1
    • claim
      failure awareness在本文被操作化为冻结feature的线性可解码性；不等于输出已校准概率、因果自省或无需监督的不确定性。class-balanced logistic分数主要验证排名和选择性预测。
      source
      S3.SS2; S4.SS2 selective prediction; S5
    • claim
      VGGT probe在encoder≤65.2%、aggregator前半63–66%，block11/12/13/15约69/81/88/93%，显示跨视图融合中段形成信号；训练自由cosine仅在VGGT后段有效，pi3不复现该简单读数。
      source
      S4.SS2 Where the signal emerges; Table4
    • claim
      短baseline平移方向是病态两视图几何：即使rotation正确，接近零的位移方向也弱可观测。其失败在特征中的可读出方向与HardMatch大视差错误不同，不能期待单一线性头跨所有失败模式。
      source
      S4.SS4, Table7

    • PCA32/64/256 AUC93.4/93.7/93.5; combining all7layers93.2 does not help. SourceTable3.

    逐项证据与原文定位
    Any3R · 完整阅读证据

    解决的问题

    前馈模型的像素索引RoPE、绝对位置与等像素训练权重暗含透视投影，换成鱼眼/全景时同一pixel并非同一射线方向或球面面积，单一内参矩阵也不能表示≥180°镜头。目标是同一权重处理混合的中央相机。

    方法 / 结构

    • 输入不是只有RGB：每视图提供从标定计算的dense unit-ray field R∈S²及有效mask M，网络不再读取lens类别/畸变系数，但要求R准确；只适用于所有射线经过单点的central cameras。
    • 沿每pixel射线预测径向距离d，解码X=dR，camera head预测camera-to-world pose；深度是radial range，不是固定z-depth。
    • 在每视图以patch平均归一化射线求elevation/azimuth，代替pixel index进行intra-frame rotary定位；绝对角度MLP也用真实ray角。
    • 最低8频用恒定角度尺度，最高8频按本视图FoV归一化；split为兼顾pose和几何的发布配置，不是所有频率都具有同样绝对角度语义。
    • 点损失在原distance^-1/2权重基础上乘每pixel球面面积，相对同view有效均值归一化；finite differences计算ray导数，1st/99th percentile clamp且weight不回传。训练混合8种camera，每view独立抽lens、2–8views/sample。

    Insight 与数学知识

    • claim
      central camera完整由R:pixel→S²描述；X=dR支持横向/后向射线。它统一已知投影，不是从未知相机自动恢复标定，也不是非中央滚动快门或多视点相机的通用证明。
      source
      S3.SS1, Equations1–2; Appendix F/G
    • claim
      角RoPE坐标为c_lat=(Hp/m)(1-2θ/π)-1、c_lon=(Wp/m)(φ/π+1)-1；代入ERP中心角恰好还原预训练索引坐标，保留已有全景先验。实际patch平均产生最多4.3% patch-step偏差。
      source
      S3.E3–E4; S3.SS2.p2.1
    • claim
      球面积分的Jacobian为dΩ=||∂R/∂u×∂R/∂v||；按有效pixel平均归一化只重分布监督、不改变总体损失量级。点损失还含每sample最优scale λ，因此不能据重建距离单位m声称输出本身绝对尺度可辨。
      source
      S3.E5–E6
    • claim
      pin-hole下dΩ=cos³ψ/f²，只在窄视角极限接近常数；90°正方形中心/角pixel面积已相差5.2倍，说明权重问题并非只存在于全景。
      source
      Appendix G narrow-pinhole limit
    • claim
      发布的频率split保留pose表现，但预注册的严格几何改进准则未满足；constant-angle arm在180/200°几何更好却pose崩溃，故是准确性目标之间的权衡。
      source
      Appendix L; Table22

    • Any3R receives calibrated rays; some baselines images only, Wid3R true ray where expressible, MapA-ray explicitly true ray. Label input differences, especially when comparing toVGGT/DA3.
    • Acc after similarity alignment and ICP does not establish metric scale recovery or pose-only reconstruction; sampled40000pixels/scene,ICP0.1m indoors/1m outdoors.
    • Main13camera benchmark has24heldout scenes/dataset and3views;8lens types seen in training,5heldout types; syntheticlens imagesresampledfrompanoramas. Reallens tests partly address this domain concern.
    • Structured3Dviews do not overlap,so pose is not scored inmain; itsdensegeometry evidence cannot be interpreted asmulti-viewalignment success.
    • Per-view conditioning gives largeposegain butM3D meanlog10deptherror0.033→0.045;solid-angleweightingdoes not improveevery metric.
    • Ablation warmstart row not samearchitecture; fullsequence AUC fractions cannot be mixedwith3-viewpercent AUC.
    • Appendix K earlierERP-only run ends withpose slightly belowpanoramicbaselineonthreeoffourdatasets; paper itself limits claimtoacross-camera robustness.
    • No独立Limitationssection; useappendix counterexamplesratherthan abstractclaimanycameraequalsallsettingsSOTA.

      逐项证据与原文定位
      HorizonStream · 完整阅读证据

      解决的问题

      流式3D中的局部对应、结构与尺度具有不同有效寿命。统一滑窗、无遗忘递归或全历史softmax会形成硬截断、状态污染和attention sinks，导致长序列漂移或崩溃；模型希望以固定状态保留多时间尺度几何。

      方法 / 结构

      • 将evidence influence kernel分解为空间content factor与时间retention factor。ViT-L继承VGGT/DINOv2，局部Gated Window Attention配合四个Geometric Linear Attention层。
      • GLA在layers4/11/17/23维护1024×1024状态，以输入条件channel-wise sigmoid decay更新；不同channel学习不同保留时间，跨chunk不reset。
      • 窗口attention按head输出门控，RoPE按(t,y,x)三轴编码。注意这里是时间+图像行列，并非从深度取得的世界xyz坐标。
      • 每帧Metric Readout Token参与状态读出，exp尺度头统一缩放translation/depth；窗口内多pose-token consensus避免仅顺序keyframe chaining。
      • 训练48帧样本按21帧计算chunk序列处理，推理pose windowW10。可选LC检索已存DINOv2global descriptor、重跑framepair、建立约束并posegraph优化；与核心严格onlinehead分开。

      Insight 与数学知识

      • claim
        核心递归S_t=diag(γ_t)S_(t-1)+φ(k_t) ṽ_tᵀ，γ_t=sigmoid(Wγx+b)；通道证据权重是∏γ，常数gate的e-fold horizon τ=-1/logγ，超过3τ权重约<5%。
        source
        S3.E5–E7; Appendix B.2
      • claim
        状态范数界||S_t||F≤γbar^t||S0||F+BkBv/(1-γbar)需要统一γbar=sup_t,c|γ|<1及key/value有界。固定张量大小保证存储不随帧数增长，但该界不证明几何误差或重定位误差永远有界。
        source
        Appendix B.3 Proposition5
      • claim
        sigmoid逐点落在(0,1)不自动提供无限序列上统一远离1的sup界；将论文条件式稳定性分析和实测长序列效果分别呈现，不能写成任意场景无限稳定保证。
        source
        Equation6 andAppendix B.3; editorial mathematical interpretation
      • claim
        softmax稀释证明假设logits有界且相关帧数Wgeo有界，相关attention总质量上界约O(Wgeo exp(2M)/t)。这是该假设下的failuremode解释，而非所有注意力记忆的普遍不可能定理。
        source
        Appendix A Proposition1
      • claim
        附录将折扣状态上的一步残差回归更新解释为TTT关联；它并未证明一般非线性网络每步精确求解累计回归最小值。核心是遗忘与固定状态的计算形式。
        source
        Appendix B.4; Equations2/5/6; editorial interpretation
      • claim
        Metric Readout使用ŝ=exp(g(zmetric))，同时缩放t和D；scale-loss只对度量样本应用。可读出scale-related signal并不等于每个慢通道单独代表尺度；probe归因跨短中长band近均匀。
        source
        S3.E9–E10;Appendix E.1

        逐项证据与原文定位
        OctWorld · 完整阅读证据

        解决的问题

        跨生成片段的世界模型在重访旧区域时容易改变几何和外观；堆叠点云会累积噪声，固定分辨率TSDF难以同时适应近景细节与不断扩展、尺度不确定的场景。本文接受首图与给定相机轨迹，研究生成世界的持久三维记忆，未估计机器人位姿。

        方法 / 结构

        OctMap以稀疏动态八叉树存TSDF、颜色和融合权重，随深度/像素覆盖选择细分层级；逐块融合生成的RGB-D，Marching Cubes提网格并渲染到未来视点，为扩散模型提供部分观测与可见性mask。FramePack压缩时间历史，Plücker射线控制相机，历史噪声增强减小训练—生成差距。当前v2同时描述FramePack/Hunyuan的RGB+深度联合模型1.0，以及Wan2.1+外部流式DA3估深的1.5；作者称1.5 depth-free仅指生成器不预测深度，整套系统仍用深度。

        Insight 与数学知识

        像素足迹给目标体素尺度 s=z/√(fx·fy)，八叉树层级体素 ℓd=L/2^d，截断半径τd∝ℓd；TSDF/颜色按 (w旧φ旧+w观测φ观测)/(w旧+w观测) 融合。空间记忆负责重访几何，压缩视频历史负责时间上下文。根节点扩展可扩大边界，但不是整套系统常数内存：所有已分配节点仍缓存GPU。1.5以token级AdaLN区分干净首帧、加噪历史和生成目标。

        实验与效果

        主文结果均为模型1.0。WorldScore静态摄影子集200场景：总体50.31，Gen3C45.48；3D一致性71.67，Gen3C70.52。Re10K最长80段正向后反向重访：LPIPS0.1633 vs Gen3C0.1933、VMem0.4010；归一化点云L1 Chamfer0.0345 vs0.0457/0.0620，非米制ATE。空间记忆消融36帧chunk：OctMap LPIPS0.1412、CD0.0252，固定TSDF0.1996/0.0345；此处与主表噪声设置不同。附录1.5总体56.02，但3D/光度一致性64.93/70.97低于1.0的71.67/77.01，体现内容对齐权衡。训练1.0用32×H100、80k低帧率+30k全36帧步骤；1.5为16×H100、DL3DV、7k步。

            复现与启发

            作者表II首chunk本方法0.136 FPS、51GB，不能把OctMap局部4–6 FPS称为整套实时。各baseline的原生分辨率、chunk长度和轨迹插值不同，VMem相机速度×4；比较是作者协议内比较。附录纠正文中‘两条433帧轨迹’：实际一条433、一条505帧；22人用户偏好53.8% vs Gen3C39.5%。当前限制：静态场景、不保留动态物体、节点持续增长/未剪枝或offload，生成实体遮挡给定未来轨迹会导致漂移。未比较arXiv v1/v2全文，不称重大修订；未运行、未下载模型/数据。不能把场景生成的重访一致性当真实SLAM定位精度；相机路径为给定输入。无动态世界记忆。模型1.5改backbone、数据、训练，不能将提升归因于一个单变量改动。

            实验与限制
            Differences between the body velocity estimated from inertial sensing and the velocity obtained from wheel odometry were evaluated using the integrated relative-velocity error, phase-derived wheel-to-body response delay, and velocity-reversal response delay. Band-integrated spectral power, spectral centroid, and spectral decay rate were extracted from each recording and evaluated using linear discriminant analysis and multiclass classification. The spectral features also provided clear separation among the tested surfaces, achieving a balanced classification accuracy of 97.1% under recording-level cross-validation. These results demonstrate that both the magnitude and frequency domain characteristics of wheel-to-body velocity mismatch contain useful information about tire-surface interactions.

            Metric Scale for Insta360 Structure-from-Motion from the Camera's Embedded IMU利用全景相机内置惯性数据对SfM轨迹执行线性尺度对齐，标定同步、旋转及偏置后按连续片段估计并平均，以消除共享偏置引起的尺度偏差。本期新文2610.35554v2仅摘要 · 未深读全文

            原文 2610.35554v2
            v1 2026-10-26T14:57:37Z · updated 2026-10-27T08:47:21Z

            方向关系
            slam-vio
            解决的问题
            Structure-from-motion (SfM) from the dual-fisheye video of a consumer 360-degree camera recovers geometry only up to a similarity transform; in the pipeline considered here, metric scale is obtained from smartphone ARKit or ARCore poses captured alongside the camera.
            方法 / 结构
            利用全景相机内置惯性数据对SfM轨迹执行线性尺度对齐，标定同步、旋转及偏置后按连续片段估计并平均，以消除共享偏置引起的尺度偏差。
            Insight 与数学
            摘要级设计洞见：利用全景相机内置惯性数据对SfM轨迹执行线性尺度对齐，标定同步、旋转及偏置后按连续片段估计并平均，以消除共享偏置引起的尺度偏差。 数学推导与成立条件未作全文核验。
            实验与限制
            The standard formulation, with gravity and accelerometer bias shared across the survey, overestimates the scale by 3.4% on average; estimating each contiguous segment independently and averaging removes the bias. On nine surveys with a co-mounted LiDAR scanner the inertial scale deviates from the LiDAR by 0.81% in mean absolute error, against 1.94% for the smartphone scale; on printed fiducial markers both agree within the 1% uncertainty of the target. On the reference trajectories of thirty sequences of a public benchmark, brought to scales of 1/40, 1 and 40, the estimator recovers the metric scale with a mean error of -0.07% and a standard deviation of 1.28%. On fifty production surveys where the smartphone is the only reference, the two scales agree to a standard deviation of 2.3%, the inertial scale being 0.3% lower on average; the difference is uncorrelated with run length, grows with the extent of the smartphone anchor, and cannot be attributed to either reference.
            证据与后续动作

            实验与限制
            The collection includes a range of challenges regarding environment, viewpoint change, and illumination conditions. (2) To address the ill-posed nature of VPR, we propose a novel approach to generate the ground-truth information in VPR benchmarks. To avoid the overhead and restrictions of evaluating VPR in-the-loop within SLAM systems, we provide VPR ground-truth (GT) matrices based on the performance of an ensemble of state-of-the-art Relative Pose Estimators (RPE). Under this GT we require a VPR method to identify exactly those image pairs for which a subsequently applied RPE is expected to provide a valuable pose estimate in a SLAM context. (3) We evaluated nine state-of-the-art VPR approaches. The results show considerable room for improvement and indicate directions for future research.

            方向关系
            geometry-foundation
            解决的问题
            Reconstructing dynamic 3D scenes over time (4D reconstruction) from a single moving camera is central to robotics and AR/VR applications, yet state-of the-art feed-forward methods rely solely on RGB frames and therefore inherit the failure modes of conventional cameras, including motion blur and temporal aliasing under fast or non-uniform camera and object motion.
            方法 / 结构
            minimal RGB/event fusion branch into Monst3R，motion sensitivity分析。
            Insight 与数学

            3benchmark平均AbsRel较RGB-only减3.4%，高物体运动收益最大；明确preliminary study。

            原文 2610.36760v1

            方向关系
            geometry-foundation
            解决的问题
            Feed-forward Gaussian Splatting enables efficient novel view synthesis, but pixel-aligned prediction makes the primitive count grow with image resolution and view count, leading to substantial redundancy and increased computational cost.
            方法 / 结构
            dual-branch Anchor/Triplane encoder；hierarchical dense point及Gaussian decoder。
            Insight 与数学
            dual-branch Anchor/Triplane encoder；hierarchical dense point及Gaussian decoder。
            实验与限制

            High-resolution 3D Gaussian Splatting training increases pixel and Gaussian-tile intersection counts, expanding rasterization caches that coexist with projection and gradient state at peak memory.

            MOON: Learning When to Forget and How to Move On in Vision-and-Language Navigation为冻结导航策略添加偏航判断器和恢复策略，在适当时刻清空会导致行为惯性的历史；仅增加少量参数并检验跨导航任务收益。本期新文2610.33546v1仅摘要 · 未深读全文

            原文 2610.33546v1
            v1 2026-10-24T08:50:29Z · updated 2026-10-24T08:50:29Z

            方向关系
            embodied-navigation
            解决的问题
            Recent vision-and-language navigation (VLN) policies act on their visual history, and an incorrect action can take them off the instructed route.
            方法 / 结构
            为冻结导航策略添加偏航判断器和恢复策略，在适当时刻清空会导致行为惯性的历史；仅增加少量参数并检验跨导航任务收益。
            Insight 与数学
            摘要级设计洞见：为冻结导航策略添加偏航判断器和恢复策略，在适当时刻清空会导致行为惯性的历史；仅增加少量参数并检验跨导航任务收益。 数学推导与成立条件未作全文核验。
            实验与限制
            We propose MOON, a framework that equips a frozen VLN policy with two lightweight modules, adding less than 1% of its parameters. A recovery policy, trained with GRPO from alarm states, learns how to move on, turning more of the successes found only by sampling into a single greedy rollout. Experiments on R2R-CE and RxR-CE show that MOON reaches the highest R2R-CE success rate among the compared methods and raises the success rate of its frozen base by 5.2 and 6.0 points, and an ablation with a random trigger indicates that this gain depends on when the history is cleared. Together, these results suggest that deviation recovery depends on knowing when to forget, not only how to act.
            证据与后续动作
            仅摘要；未深读全文、未运行。若后续深读，核验Method、Experiments、消融与官方实现；本轮未本地运行。

            方向关系
            embodied-navigation
            解决的问题
            Autonomous navigation in off-road terrain is commonly achieved using hierarchical control and planning architectures that rely on explicit cost maps and one-way information flow from a high-level planner to a low-level controller, decoupling planning from the true execution cost experienced during control.
            方法 / 结构
            用低层强化学习控制器的价值函数代替显式代价图指导上层路径积分轨迹搜索，再在线适配价值评价以应对未见越野地形。
            Insight 与数学
            摘要级设计洞见：用低层强化学习控制器的价值函数代替显式代价图指导上层路径积分轨迹搜索，再在线适配价值评价以应对未见越野地形。 数学推导与成立条件未作全文核验。
            实验与限制
            Autonomous navigation in off-road terrain is commonly achieved using hierarchical control and planning architectures that rely on explicit cost maps and one-way information flow from a high-level planner to a low-level controller, decoupling planning from the true execution cost experienced during control. To account for the distribution shift seen by the value function between training and evaluation, we propose an online policy evaluation process that adapts the value function to unseen driving experiences, and we demonstrate this adaptation in unseen off-road simulation environments. We evaluate our approach in a high-fidelity off-road driving simulator across a range of challenging terrain conditions and compare its performance against a baseline MPPI controller. The results demonstrate that our method achieves safer and more effective navigation to goal locations while eliminating the need for explicit cost maps and enabling tighter coupling between planning and control.

            解决的问题

            We introduce WMEval, a comprehensive benchmark that evaluates interactive world models against Unity-based physical simulations. By executing matched action sequences in both the simulator and the world model, WMEval enables evaluation at both the perceptual level and the physical-causal level, using simulator-derived states as reference. Experiments on state-of-the-art models reveal significant room for improvement in dimensions that remain hidden by standard perceptual metrics but are vital for real-world deployments.

            However, existing benchmarks largely evaluate isolated capabilities, failing to capture the complexity of open-world robot autonomy. We introduce RobotAgentBench (RAB), a comprehensive benchmark for evaluating robot agents in realistic, open-world household environments. RAB provides a multi-level evaluation of robot agent capabilities: (1) task-level assessments systematically examine five complementary dimensions: task discovery, context reasoning, manipulation, failure recovery, and robustness; (2) scenario-level evaluation assesses how agents integrate these capabilities to autonomously fulfill high-level user needs. Extensive experiments reveal substantial gaps between manipulation and autonomy, demonstrate the advantages of agentic architectures and recursive self-improvement, and highlight the remaining challenges toward open-world robot autonomy.

In this paper, we show that it is possible to achieve the best of both worlds: a latent geometry optimized to make future prediction as simple as possible without sacrificing accurate prediction of future observations. The gains include up to 5x cheaper inference, over 7x faster training, and 15-24% lower pixel and perceptual errors out of distribution and over long horizons. Our world models also agree more closely with real-world policy evaluation when using a vision-language model to judge task success from their pixel-space predictions.

Our results suggest that test-time adaptation of world models for planning benefits from an optimistic, task-aware term in addition to the prediction loss.

            实验与限制
            It has four tasks scored as five families, 138 objects in seen, unseen-instance and unseen-class splits, 916 evaluation episodes with subsets for held-out appearances, rotate-to-fit planning and occlusion memory, and 3,629 simulated demonstrations on which every learned policy is fine-tuned under an equal compute budget. The twin ranks policies as the real robot does: five policies trained without real data, four VLAs and an LLM agent, keep their exact ranking when the same weights run on the physical arm in an unseen environment with unseen objects (Spearman rho = 1.00, MMRV = 0.000), although simulation overestimates success. Across nine VLAs and world-action models, the best learned policies score near 61%, with spatial placement, unseen object classes and rotate-to-fit planning remaining open. An LLM agent that writes code and hands grasping to a learned policy scores 83.2%, and an ablation shows that this lead comes from the choice of harness and the choice of language model.

            解决的问题

            方法 / 结构
            识别离线轨迹中高方差分叉节点，分段学习目标条件图边以避免随机性导致的虚假可达路径，在长期目标控制环境检验可靠到达。
            Insight 与数学
            摘要级设计洞见：识别离线轨迹中高方差分叉节点，分段学习目标条件图边以避免随机性导致的虚假可达路径，在长期目标控制环境检验可靠到达。 数学推导与成立条件未作全文核验。
            实验与限制
            While prior methods have sought to address this challenge through various approaches, a key challenge remains path mirage, where agents overcommit to spuriously successful trajectories in offline datasets induced by stochasticity, often leading to failure on difficult tasks.             解决的问题
            We introduce WorldOracle, a benchmark specifically designed to evaluate action-conditioned world models (ACWMs) against paired real-world executions.
            方法 / 结构
            把同一初始观测和低层动作的预测滚动与真实执行配对，从物理状态、交互位置、关键区域和多视图一致性评价机器人世界模型。
            Insight 与数学
            摘要级设计洞见：把同一初始观测和低层动作的预测滚动与真实执行配对，从物理状态、交互位置、关键区域和多视图一致性评价机器人世界模型。 数学推导与成立条件未作全文核验。
            实验与限制
            To support this evaluation, we curate real-world training and evaluation suites that cover different interaction dynamics and embodiments. The evaluation suite contains long, multi-segment episodes with both successful and failed executions, enabling evaluation across varied action outcomes and behavior distributions. Experiments on seven representative ACWMs reveal distinct model strengths and failure modes. We release the datasets, evaluation code, and leaderboard: https://worldoracle-benchmark.github.io/.

            方向关系
            world-model
            解决的问题
            Bimanual manipulation with dexterous hands has attracted growing attention in research on robotic action models.
            方法 / 结构
            以多自由度双手仿真构建百余任务和受控空间视觉变化，统一比较多种机器人动作模型在泛化、指令、小样本、长程和记忆方面的能力。
            Insight 与数学
            摘要级设计洞见：以多自由度双手仿真构建百余任务和受控空间视觉变化，统一比较多种机器人动作模型在泛化、指令、小样本、长程和记忆方面的能力。 数学推导与成立条件未作全文核验。
            实验与限制
            In this paper, we present BiDex-100, a simulation benchmark for bimanual dexterous manipulation that combines broad task coverage, multi-dimensional evaluation, and high-DoF hand embodiments. Concretely, the benchmark comprises 106 tasks organized along five capability axes: generalization, instruction following, limited demonstrations, long horizon, and memory. In addition, the benchmark supports two embodiments and provides 8,080 success-validated teleoperated demonstrations, totaling 6.25 million frames. We evaluate seven recent Vision-Language-Action models under a unified protocol, establishing baselines for capability-specific performance, robustness, and data efficiency.

            方法 / 结构
            以状态和动作单因子仍在训练支持内、但组合未充分出现的目标变体检验世界模型，分离预测动力学和规划在组合泛化下的失败。

            摘要级设计洞见：以状态和动作单因子仍在训练支持内、但组合未充分出现的目标变体检验世界模型，分离预测动力学和规划在组合泛化下的失败。 数学推导与成立条件未作全文核验。
            实验与限制
            To answer this, we introduce AnyGoal, a benchmark of goal-shifted task variants built from widely used world-model environments, together with an open-source library. By examining dynamics and representation failures across model families, interaction regimes, and evaluation metrics, we study where current world models generalize and where challenges remain for goal-shifted prediction and planning. Together, this framework, benchmark, and analysis provide a basis for evaluating progress toward world models that generalize to new planning tasks.

To evaluate the entity correspondence, we propose an entity-level benchmark of 59 held-out cases with 753 entities, scored against ground-truth boxes. Compared with the base model, AnyGameRenderer better preserves entity correspondence: temporal match rises from 50.9% to 62.9%, missed count changes fall from 34.7% to 21.3%, and fewer entities are hallucinated or left unrendered.

            Experiments on RoboTwin 2.0 first demonstrate the effectiveness of E0.5 without embodied-data pretraining, achieving success rates of 89.90\% and 50.09\% under clean and randomized settings, respectively.             方向关系
            world-action
            解决的问题
            World Action Models (WAMs) combine future-video prediction with robot action learning, yet improvements in task success alone do not reveal how predictive supervision manifests in execution behavior or whether its benefits persist under task and environmental shifts.
            方法 / 结构
            在固定架构和训练配置下消融未来视频监督，区分熟悉任务执行改进与分布外迁移，并检验无需大型生成骨干的轻量前瞻辅助训练。
            Insight 与数学
            摘要级设计洞见：在固定架构和训练配置下消融未来视频监督，区分熟悉任务执行改进与分布外迁移，并检验无需大型生成骨干的轻量前瞻辅助训练。 数学推导与成立条件未作全文核验。
            实验与限制
            Trajectory clustering further reveals higher success rates within several coarse trajectory clusters, including cases where cluster frequencies change little, indicating that the gains are not solely reflected in changes in the prevalence of trajectory patterns. However, these benefits do not consistently persist under distribution shifts: improvements on unseen tasks vary across models and tasks, while both models exhibit low success rates under the evaluated environmental randomizations, without a consistent advantage from predictive supervision. We further introduce future-video prediction as an auxiliary training objective for $π_{0.5}$, where a lightweight Transformer predictor used only during training increases success from 82.94% to 89.19%. These findings support future-video prediction as effective auxiliary supervision for action learning, while distinguishing improved execution on familiar tasks from reliable transfer and showing that such gains need not rely on a large pretrained video diffusion transformer (DiT) in the evaluated setting.

            解决的问题
            Frontier multimodal large language models (MLLMs) can control robots without robot-specific training, but how their capabilities compare and interact with post-trained vision-language-action (VLA) and world action model (WAM) policies across manipulation demands remains to be thoroughly evaluated.

            实验与限制
            Frontier multimodal large language models (MLLMs) can control robots without robot-specific training, but how their capabilities compare and interact with post-trained vision-language-action (VLA) and world action model (WAM) policies across manipulation demands remains to be thoroughly evaluated. In this report, we evaluate GPT-6-Astra on EBench as the core of an agent that controls a robot through an agent harness under the same visual and proprioceptive observation protocol as post-trained policies. Under direct control, the agent achieves competitive overall performance relative to post-trained policies. In the harness with policy, invoking a post-trained VLA or WAM combines the complementary strengths of both components, raising overall task success to the level of the strongest compared post-trained policies while substantially reducing token cost.

            However, they still require tens of denoising steps to achieve competitive action quality, incurring substantial latency and hindering real-time deployment.             Rollback: Experience Backtracking in World Models for Policy Improvement在世界模型中回到关键历史决策点分叉比较替代动作，将反事实结果蒸馏为动作监督，并用真实机器人转移持续校正轻量动力学适配器。本期新文2610.36068v1仅摘要 · 未深读全文

            方向关系
            world-action
            解决的问题
            Action-conditioned world models offer a scalable alternative to costly real-world interaction for improving robot policies.
            方法 / 结构
            在世界模型中回到关键历史决策点分叉比较替代动作，将反事实结果蒸馏为动作监督，并用真实机器人转移持续校正轻量动力学适配器。
            Insight 与数学
            摘要级设计洞见：在世界模型中回到关键历史决策点分叉比较替代动作，将反事实结果蒸馏为动作监督，并用真实机器人转移持续校正轻量动力学适配器。 数学推导与成立条件未作全文核验。
            实验与限制
            We introduce Rollback, a policy improvement paradigm that turns world models from passive simulators into counterfactual engines: it rolls back to critical decision points, branches into alternative actions, and contrasts their outcomes to attribute success to individual decisions. Experiments on three challenging real-world tasks show that Rollback improves success rates over the strongest baseline by an absolute 10.67%, 11.11%, and 11.85% on block extraction, ball tossing, and tabletop curling, respectively, while achieving more stable improvement throughout training. Although initialized from teleoperated demonstrations far below human level, Rollback surpasses the average success rate of human players on both ball tossing and tabletop curling within 8 hours of training.

            原文 2610.37196v1
            v1 2026-10-27T13:17:18Z · updated 2026-10-27T13:17:18Z

            方向关系
            world-action
            解决的问题
            World action models (WAMs) predict the visual consequences of the actions they generate, which makes test-time selection a natural way to improve a fixed policy: imagine the futures of candidate actions and execute the best one.
            方法 / 结构
            通过可复现反事实分叉和重复后续执行去噪估计多候选选择空间，再用失败门控选择器只在必要时偏离原动作块，评价真实闭环收益。
            Insight 与数学
            摘要级设计洞见：通过可复现反事实分叉和重复后续执行去噪估计多候选选择空间，再用失败门控选择器只在必要时偏离原动作块，评价真实闭环收益。 数学推导与成立条件未作全文核验。
            实验与限制
            Across four WAMs, two benchmarks, over 100,000 branched rollouts, and pre-registered tests, we find that (i) the standard estimate overstates the selectable headroom of native samples: bounds from 17 continuations per candidate place its selectable share between 24% and 66% for three WAMs; (ii) structured action perturbations create up to 16 points of selectable headroom; and (iii) in both WAMs with action-conditioned imagination, imagined futures carry candidate-outcome information with no detectable loss relative to real futures, information that the value head of Cosmos Policy leaves unused. Deviating from the policy chunk only when a readout of the WAM predicts failure, the selector raises closed-loop task success from 76.9% to 81.6% on held-out episodes of training tasks, with the largest gain where the policy fails most often, and from 62.6% to 69.8% on unseen tasks.

            Across 20 dual-arm tasks from RoboTwin~V2 and BiCoord with three execution backbones, LOGIV improves mean task success without injected disturbances by 10.67 and 15.33 percentage points, respectively.             world-action
            解决的问题
            Choosing a robot action model requires evidence about task capability, adaptation cost, deployment requirements, and the reliability of reported conclusions.
            方法 / 结构
            将机器人能力、训练及部署成本、本地研究价值和复现证据分维评价，使用固定归一化尺度并保留缺测，以支持不同预算下的模型选择。
            Insight 与数学
            摘要级设计洞见：将机器人能力、训练及部署成本、本地研究价值和复现证据分维评价，使用固定归一化尺度并保留缺测，以支持不同预算下的模型选择。 数学推导与成立条件未作全文核验。
            实验与限制
            Success rates alone offer limited guidance to researchers with different computing budgets and goals. We propose RoboSeed, a multidimensional evaluation framework for vision-language-action (VLA) and world-action models (WAM), within a continuing program to train, evaluate, and openly release robot models with verifiable evidence for model selection. We apply the framework to representative VLA and WAM models, drawing on local training, released-weight evaluation, task-level audits, and an evaluation-system study. The results reveal budget-dependent backbone choices, sensitivity to adaptation recipes, and differences between language and visual task interfaces.

            由少量人类玩耍示教训练自主采集策略，再用所得交互学习世界模型并指导预测控制，同时在模型中自玩蒸馏目标策略以加速灵巧操作。
            Insight 与数学
            摘要级设计洞见：由少量人类玩耍示教训练自主采集策略，再用所得交互学习世界模型并指导预测控制，同时在模型中自玩蒸馏目标策略以加速灵巧操作。 数学推导与成立条件未作全文核验。
            实验与限制
            During inference, the same play policy then guides model-predictive control (MPC) by proposing candidate action sequences for evaluation under the learned world model. We evaluate this framework on underactuated pushing and any-to-any in-hand pose reorientation with a multifingered hand, in simulation and real world. In real world experiment, our method outperforms hindsight behavior cloning by 69.6% in success rate and achieves comparable success to reinforcement learning with 490 times less interaction data. These results demonstrate that supervised play can autonomously collect interaction data, guide model-based planning, and enable policy learning without any task-specific demonstrations or sim-to-real transfer.

            以交织视频语言推理统一具身理解和长程视觉规划，再通过强化学习选择视频预测，并扩展成世界动作控制以评估复合机器人任务。

            To evaluate these capabilities, we introduce Rynn-VP and Rynn-GEU for long-horizon visual planning and anticipatory reasoning. Experiments show that RynnUnify achieves the highest average embodied understanding score among the evaluated unified multimodal models and improves the Rynn-VP overall score by 9.75 points over Cosmos 3 Nano. Its agentic extension improves the Rynn-GEU average by 6.0 points over direct reasoning, while the world-action extension achieves 51.5\% success on 20 selected composite RoboCasa365 tasks and 46.7\% average success across three real-robot tasks. Together, these results establish RynnUnify as a shared foundation for embodied understanding, visual planning, and robot control, taking a step toward a unified embodied brain.

            方向关系
            world-action
            解决的问题
            Robot foundation models are typically evaluated through costly closed-loop rollouts, motivating low-cost offline proxies for model capability.
            方法 / 结构
            以感知表示和物理运动轨迹结构的对齐程度构造无需训练的离线指标，检验其与机器人成功率及视觉语言骨干可迁移能力的关系。
            Insight 与数学
            摘要级设计洞见：以感知表示和物理运动轨迹结构的对齐程度构造无需训练的离线指标，检验其与机器人成功率及视觉语言骨干可迁移能力的关系。 数学推导与成立条件未作全文核验。
            实验与限制
            Robot foundation models are typically evaluated through costly closed-loop rollouts, motivating low-cost offline proxies for model capability. We find that $S^2$ correlates positively with downstream robot task success across model families, making it a low-cost offline proxy for prioritizing models before closed-loop evaluation. $S^2$ can also identify promising vision-language model (VLM) backbones before costly fine-tuning into vision-language-action models (VLAs). With $S^2$, we extend PRH to robotics and make sensorimotor alignment measurable, offering a low-cost, representation-level view of robot foundation models that closed-loop success rates alone cannot reveal.

            把磁异常地图匹配写成链式因子图，用分段前向后向推断保留位置后验及香农熵，同时以熵诊断无真值条件下的地图定位歧义。
            Insight 与数学
            摘要级设计洞见：把磁异常地图匹配写成链式因子图，用分段前向后向推断保留位置后验及香农熵，同时以熵诊断无真值条件下的地图定位歧义。 数学推导与成立条件未作全文核验。
            实验与限制
            Over the \texttt{Renfrew\_395} map, $10^3$ synthetic trials yield a median position error of $32.7$ m, $91\%$ below the median inertial drift of $362$ m. On these trials and $10^3$ flight paths, the median error is $43\%$ below the extended Kalman filter and particle filter. Across these paths, a threshold on segment-level entropy detects $47\%$ of segments with errors above two map cells and $94\%$ of those above three, at a $10\%$ false-alarm rate. The foregoing results indicate that $H_t$ provides a truth-free diagnostic of map-conditioned navigation ambiguity.

            解决的问题
            Collecting naturalistic human manipulation demonstrations with accurate contact force and hand pose is essential for robot dexterous manipulation policy and foundational world model learning - yet no existing wearable platform provides both calibrated fingertip force sensing and occlusion-resilient hand tracking in a form factor that preserves natural manipulation behavior.
            方法 / 结构
            将薄型触觉手套、标定力传感及惯性融合手姿态追踪结合，在遮挡操作中同时记录接触和运动，为接触感知机器人世界模型提供数据。
            Insight 与数学
            摘要级设计洞见：将薄型触觉手套、标定力传感及惯性融合手姿态追踪结合，在遮挡操作中同时记录接触和运动，为接触感知机器人世界模型提供数据。 数学推导与成立条件未作全文核验。
            实验与限制
            To enable objective comparison, we introduce standardized benchmarking protocols for tactile force regression and hand pose estimation, and apply them to Tactus and two commercially available tactile gloves - to our knowledge the first quantitative cross-platform evaluation of this kind. Tactus resolves a minimum detectable force of 0.59 N at 95% confidence, 1.6 - 2.7x lower than the commercial baselines, and with 0.87 cross-participant consistency, while our pose tracking sustains 11.0 mm fingertip keypoint relative error during manipulation, when occlusion is heaviest.

            To isolate the role of representation in spatial imagination, we introduce ImagiRep, a simulator-grounded benchmark of 922 questions spanning seven question types across two settings, Viewpoint Imagination and Cross-view Integration, providing oracle RGB and six alternative representations (e.g., depth, panoramic views, and top-down geometry) of the same unobserved state.             ProactEgoMem: Benchmarking Proactive Memory in Streaming Egocentric Video在流式自我中心视频中评价主动捕获信息、跟踪对象状态和位置的记忆维护，分别检查及时通知与主动工具生命周期，而非只问被动最终答案。旧文更新2610.30089v2仅摘要 · 未深读全文

            v1 2026-10-21T17:37:58Z · updated 2026-10-26T16:19:38Z

            方向关系
            spatial-understanding
            解决的问题
            Today's multimodal assistants are typically prompted to interpret the visual evidence presented to them.
            方法 / 结构
            在流式自我中心视频中评价主动捕获信息、跟踪对象状态和位置的记忆维护，分别检查及时通知与主动工具生命周期，而非只问被动最终答案。
            Insight 与数学
            摘要级设计洞见：在流式自我中心视频中评价主动捕获信息、跟踪对象状态和位置的记忆维护，分别检查及时通知与主动工具生命周期，而非只问被动最终答案。 数学推导与成立条件未作全文核验。
            实验与限制
            We introduce ProactEgoMem, a benchmark that evaluates proactive memory in streaming egocentric video. Three tasks ground the evaluation in ambient environmental needs: Information Capture (IC) preserves transient yet dense visual information, State Tracking (ST) follows changing object states, and Location Tracking (LT) records object movements. The benchmark defines its protocol at two levels of proactiveness: the Notification Level asks for a single timely notification that satisfies the user's intent, while the deeper Intervention Level asks the agent to maintain memory on the user's behalf through an active start-to-stop tool lifecycle. ProactEgoMem provides a rigorous evaluation foundation for proactive multimodal assistance and for video agents that co-exist with their users.

            方向关系
            spatial-understanding
            解决的问题
            Coding agents are rapidly advancing from single-file generation to long-horizon repository-scale engineering, yet the evaluation of their capabilities to author interactive extended reality (XR) applications remains an open challenge.
            方法 / 结构
            以消费者XR硬件上的动态三维输入和环境感知测试编码代理，结合脚本及自适应交互检查物理落地、传感生命周期和应用运行可靠性。
            Insight 与数学
            摘要级设计洞见：以消费者XR硬件上的动态三维输入和环境感知测试编码代理，结合脚本及自适应交互检查物理落地、传感生命周期和应用运行可靠性。 数学推导与成立条件未作全文核验。
            实验与限制
            Held-out tests combine scripted and adaptive interactions with programmatic and visual assertions to evaluate core functionality and runtime behavior. GenXR-Bench's testing suite achieves 96.5% accuracy against blinded expert judgments of 124 candidate applications executed on consumer XR hardware. Evaluating 10 models on GenXR-Bench, we find that the strongest configuration achieves only 50.9% overall Pass@1. GenXR-Bench establishes a reproducible testbed for evaluating coding agents in the interactive and physically situated conditions of spatial computing.

            原文 2610.32177v1
            v1 2026-10-23T05:24:02Z · updated 2026-10-23T05:24:02Z

            方向关系
            spatial-understanding
            解决的问题
            Humans reason about the physical world by flexibly combining different forms of inference as a problem unfolds.
            方法 / 结构
            通过共享标记接口交织语言、图像、几何和运动中间表示，并用任务反馈强化学习选择推理形式，评价多视图和反事实空间理解。
            Insight 与数学
            摘要级设计洞见：通过共享标记接口交织语言、图像、几何和运动中间表示，并用任务反馈强化学习选择推理形式，评价多视图和反事实空间理解。 数学推导与成立条件未作全文核验。
            实验与限制
            Achieving this flexibility is a major open challenge in AI: capabilities often remain separated across specialized models, limiting how they can be composed and jointly adapted to a task. Reinforcement learning from task feedback jointly optimizes the selection, generation, and use of intermediate representations, improving visual question-answering accuracy with gains that transfer to unseen benchmarks. $Ψ$-0.6 offers a path toward adaptive physical reasoning -- not only predicting the physical world but also choosing the representations through which to reason.
            证据与后续动作
            仅摘要；未深读全文、未运行。若后续深读，核验Method、Experiments、消融与官方实现；本轮未本地运行。

            Trends & Insights

            1. 几何状态与实时路径需要分开评估

            RESPLE2的递归状态、可选全局修正和HorizonStream的核心循环状态有不同资源边界。报告保留端点误差、整轨迹误差、局部状态内存和回环开销的区别。

            2. 统一相机与可用置信度是两种不同问题

            Any3R以标定射线描述像素几何，不能据此推断不需标定；VGGT失败探测研究的是预测能否识别失效，不能把原始置信度默认视为校准后的可靠性。

            3. 世界模型需要显式报告几何代价

            OctWorld长期轨迹受静态场景和GPU地图增长限制；DA3深度仍在模型1.5管线中。模块速度、完整生成FPS和下游导航能力应各自给证据。

            4. 热闹但证据不足的方向

            摘要级世界动作、导航和机器人应用已完整跟踪，但未据摘要作统一优劣排名。441条摘要跟踪的具体协议、统计显著性、权重可用性与复现均保留逐ID待办。
